485-## AUDITORÍA MATEMÁTICA FINANCIERA (2026-07-21) — la aritmética replica; el REPORTE tenía 3 sesgos optimistas
486-
487:Recálculos independientes: composición 2025/2022-24/2026, maxDD, PF, p-value — **todos
488-replican al 4º decimal** ✓. Los hallazgos son de matemática financiera, no de aritmética:
489-
490-1. **Sharpe 1.506 está inflado por construcción**: es solo-trades (34 semanas), sin
491-   risk-free. Con las 18 semanas flat = 1.21; en exceso de rf USD 4.37% = **0.80**.
492-   El skill performance-metrics exige (Rp−Rf)/σ. Todo titular futuro publica el de exceso.
493-2. **El fill del HS es una orden imposible**: el yaml declara `hard_stop: "limit"` — un
494:   buy-limit al nivel del stop NO se ejecuta en gap-through. Medido 2025: trade 7 abrió
495-   14bps más allá del stop → corrección −0.28pp; con slippage intradía realista
496-   −0.3/−1.0pp/año y DD mayor. Favorece estructuralmente a v12 (menos HS). El yaml debe
497-   decir stop-market y el motor llenar open-aware (re-medición, 0 trials).
498-3. **El forward 2026 está inflado por carry omitido**: 10L/1S pagan diferencial —
499-   recalculado −0.24pp (el +3.36% purgado sería ≈+3.12% con pass-through completo).
500-   El único juez limpio tiene sesgo pro-LONG hasta que el motor devengue carry
501-   bidireccional (gate: H-COP-CARRY-00).
502-4. Carry teórico corregido: **+1.83/+1.91pp** (no +2.01: IBR es E.A. vs FFR nominal
503-   act/360 → diferencial 4.27pp; 98 noches, no 103 días).
504-5. **La palanca más grande del track no es de señal: colateral ocioso = +4.3-4.5pp/año**
505-   (18 semanas 100% flat + margen no usado). Es elección de venue/broker declarable
506-   ex-ante (0 trials). En venue sin interés (MEXC/USDT) ≈ 0 → entonces el Sharpe DEBE
507-   reportarse en exceso de cash (≈0.8).
508-6. Kelly guard: riesgo actual = 0.23×Kelly (v12 ≈ 0.17×) — no hay upside legal en subir
509-   tamaño; el debate 2.0 vs 1.5 vive dentro de la zona fraccional segura.
510:7. Calmar a cierres diarios = 1.69 ✓ defendible; el multi-año honesto (v12 2022-25:
511:   CAGR 5.2%, Calmar ≤0.88) es el número comparable con CTAs. vt_min=0.5 anula los
512-   multiplicadores de de-risking (candidato a spec v12 o +1 trial).

556-Codex exec-audit) convergieron con cálculos separados. Números triangulados:
557-
558-- **Techo de detectabilidad**: MDE a N=300 semanas = DA≥57.2% / IC≥0.143 (con maxT
559-  familia-6: IC≥0.148-0.20) vs IC plausible de macro semanal 0.02-0.05. Potencia real
560-  8-22%. Verificación de IC=0.05: **47.6-49.5 años** (dos cálculos independientes).
561-- **El ruido gana**: E[max IC espurio] con 6 candidatas ≈ 0.11 — 2.5× el efecto buscado.
562-  "Si una feature sobrevive el screen, la explicación más probable es leakage."
563-- **Fundamental Law**: con BR efectivo 34 y TC~0.6, mover el Calmar exige IC≥0.086 —
564-  fuera del rango institucional para un solo par EM de sesión 5h.
565-- **Valor de +1pt DA ≈ +1.9pp/año** — y es incertificable (297 años para confirmar +1pt).
566-
567-**Ranking unánime del dinero**: (1) colateral remunerado +4.3-4.5pp [0 trials, venue];
568-(2) carry ejecutable +1.0-1.9pp [gate H-COP-CARRY-00, statements]; (3) ejecución honesta
569-(stop-market open-aware, carry bidireccional, costos ×2/×3 con dientes); (4) forward
570:congelado v11/v12 sin reutilizar 2025; (5) **comprar N, no columnas** (historia mensual
571-larga PIT — forwards BanRep desde 1997, remesas desde 2000 — y eventual panel LatAm);
572-(6) al final, UN experimento pre-registrado de carry/flujo como tilt/gate.
573-

575-(cantidad/flujo, información nueva; condición dura: vintage PIT; Codex precisa que los
576-forwards BanRep son MENSUALES desde 1997 → el estudio legal es mensual de carry/curva,

578-refutados K3/K4), carbón (3er intento del canal ToT), remesas (~60 datos únicos, contenido
579-de sorpresa ≈0 a 5d), carry real (canal ya cubierto por H-CARRY-01; infexp-EOF mejora el

794:## H-V13-QRISK-01 (PRE-REGISTRO 2026-07-21, 0 miradas) — techo de leverage probabilístico
795-
796-**Fórmula SELLADA (cero grid; cada constante con su prior declarado):**
797:- `q90_hat_t` = 0.5·persistencia(q90 rolling-252 del rango 5d) + 0.5·QR(q90) sobre las
798-  4 features ganadoras de H-RISK-FAM-01 {vol-of-vol, gap-cola, EMBI-acel, RESINT},
799-  ajustada walk-forward SOLO con datos < t (composición 50/50: el listón imbatido ancla,
800-  las features validadas modulan — sin pesos optimizados).
801:- `techo_t = 1.5 × clip(mediana_diseño(q90_hat) / q90_hat_t, 0.5, 1.0)` — riesgo predicho
802:  sobre la mediana ⇒ el techo baja proporcionalmente; JAMÁS sube de 1.5 (v13 ⊂ v12).
803-- Todo lo demás idéntico a v12. Ventanas canónicas del operador (diseño 2020/22-24).
804-- **Design-run pareado** (v12 vs v13, mismas señales): abre +1 trial (N 66→67).
805:  Aprobación por diseño = Calmar_v13 ≥ Calmar_v12 en 2022-2024 (replay de salidas con
806-  effective-HS recalculado por leverage). **Juez real = forward** desde su freeze,
807-  mismo protocolo sellado de v12 (reloj propio, corte-52).

862-

864-perfecta, lev 1.5, costos 1bp/lado — `.claude/evidence/cop_oracle/2026-07-21/`):

870-
871-**Lecturas:**
872-1. **El 20-30% SÍ existe dentro de la clase de mecánica** (bastaría capturar 17-25% del
873-   techo vs el 6% actual) — la meta es geométricamente alcanzable sin cambiar la clase
874-   de entrada. La restricción no es la señal: es CUÁNTO se deja correr al ganador.
875-2. Los bolsillos grandes (top semanas 3.7-6.0pp disponibles) mueren hoy en el TP fijo
876-   ~1.6%: el TP captura la cola corta de semanas que ofrecían 3× más.
877-3. **PROHIBICIÓN explícita** (constitución §1 + evento de hoy): entrenar CUALQUIER modelo
878:   por prueba-y-error contra estos puntos de entrada/salida 2025 = imitación del oráculo
879-   = la forma pura del overfitting que hoy vimos colapsar el diseño a −17%. El oráculo
880-   es mapa de techo, jamás target de entrenamiento.
881-
882-**H-TP-LADDER-01 (PLANNED, la hipótesis mecánica que el diagnóstico habilita)**: salida
883-escalonada pre-declarada — 50% del tamaño al TP actual (vol-escalado ~1.6%), 50% restante
884-al 2×TP o week-end, mismo HS. UNA variable (estructura de salida), prior ex-ante (el
885-oráculo muestra bolsillos 2-3× el TP; el skill em-fx: dejar correr en EM con stop de
886:tamaño), diseño en motor 2021-2024 pareado vs v12 + re-medición 2025 de la misma celda,
887-juez = forward. Costo: +1 trial al abrir. Nota: revisa el DO-NOT histórico "no trailing"
888-— esto NO es trailing (es ladder fijo); si el operador la aprueba, es la siguiente en cola.

904-
905:**Las decisiones que el 30% de 2025 exigía**: (1) salida escalonada dejando correr la
906-mitad al 2×TP (+1.5pp por sí sola — confirma el diagnóstico del oráculo, base de
907-H-TP-LADDER-01); (2) **leverage ~3-4×** — y ahí está el precio: el 30% pleno requería
908:lev ~4.5 con DD proyectado 15-18%, y por simetría el 2022 (−9% a lev 2) habría sido
909:−18/−25%. El 30% de 2025 era una decisión de APALANCAMIENTO, no de señal.
910-
911-**2026 es el veredicto estructural**: ni la MEJOR política de la clase (S10) pasa de
912-+6.5% YTD vs los +16% del ritmo-30% — con el gate permitiendo 11 trades en un año
913-mean-reverting, el ritmo 30% NO EXISTÍA en un solo par. Confirmación empírica de la
914-Fundamental Law del panel: el 30% sostenido exige breadth (más sleeves), no más presión
915-sobre COP. Prohibido desplegar la celda ganadora por estos números; H-TP-LADDER-01 sigue
916-su cauce (motor + forward) y el apalancamiento del LIBRO (no de la pata) es la vía al
917-objetivo. 0 trials (mapa de decisiones, sin selección de despliegue).

945-La config del +20.11% del contrafactual (ladder + cap 3.0) corrida en el MOTOR real:

948-|---|---|---|---|

950:| 2022 | −7.64% | −7.98% | 5 |
951-| 2023 | −1.26% | −0.93% | 5 |

953-| **Compuesto 21-24** | **−14.0%** | −6.3% | |
954:| 2025 | **+12.77%** (la sim prometía +20.11%) | +6.59% | 5 |
955-
956-**Dos razones por las que el 20% era espejismo:**
957-1. **El simulador crudo ignoraba la dinámica del HS efectivo**: a lev 3 el buffer de
958-   precio se comprime a 3.5%/3 ≈ 1.17% — semanas que a lev 1.5 sobrevivían hasta el TP
959:   mueren en −3.5% de equity. 2024 VOLTEA de +4.6% a −2.66% (4 hard stops nuevos). El
960-   +20.11% de la sim vale +12.77% en ejecución honesta.
961-2. **El diseño paga −14% compuesto** — peor que v14 en la ventana limpia. El leverage
962-   sobre ESTA estrategia amplifica la parte que no tiene edge junto con la que sí.
963-
964-**Conclusión sellada**: el apalancamiento no es una variable de diseño de la pata — es
965-una decisión de CAPITAL sobre lo graduado (a nivel libro, post-forward, con guard
966-fraccional-Kelly). La escalera (v14) se queda; el lev alto en la pata queda cerrado con
967-evidencia de motor. Celda reportada completa, no desplegable.

993-## H-CHRONOS-01 (PRE-REGISTRO 2026-07-22, 0 miradas) — fundacional zero-shot → q90 rango semanal COP
994-
995-Diseño consolidado Claude+Codex (revisión adversarial R1-R6, `codex exec -p audit`,
996-artefacto del review en `.claude/evidence/cop_chronos/`). Aprobado por el operador.
997-
998-**Hipótesis**: `amazon/chronos-bolt-base` zero-shot (SIN fine-tuning) predice el q90 del
999-rango de las próximas 5 sesiones mejor que la persistencia rolling-252. Mejora candidata
1000-al SIZING, nunca a la dirección.
1001-
1002-**Diseño SELLADO (15 puntos Codex, todos los grados de libertad clavados ex-ante):**
1003-1. Modelo único: `amazon/chronos-bolt-base`, revisión = último commit HF con fecha
1004:   < 2025-01-01 (publicación ≠ cutoff del corpus; el checkpoint precede al test).
1005-2. Sin fine-tuning, sin calibración de hiperparámetros, sin modelos alternativos.
1006-   **TimesFM prohibido incluso si Chronos pierde.**
1007-3. Serie base: rango de sesión % = (max(high)−min(low))/close_última ×100 desde M5,
1008-   sesiones con ≥30 barras (constructor idéntico a H-VOLT-01). Escala definida UNA vez.
1009-4. Target: y_t = max(rng_{t+1..t+5}) sobre las próximas 5 sesiones ELEGIBLES (no días
1010-   calendario).
1011-5. Anti off-by-one: en el origen t solo son observables etiquetas hasta y_{t−5}.
1012-6. Input univariado: historial causal de y (hasta y_{t−5}); contexto = máximo soportado
1013-   por el modelo, truncado por la izquierda.
1014-7. Horizon=5; se lee el canal q90 nativo del paso 5 (= y_t). SIN sample paths (los
1015-   cuantiles de Bolt son marginales; máximos desde marginales = inválido — Codex R2).
1016:8. TEST primario: 2025, UN disparo (orígenes con fecha en 2025; enero-2026 solo para
1017-   realizar etiquetas de diciembre, jamás como input). 2026 YTD solo descriptivo.
1018-   Confirmación real = forward post-freeze.
1019-9. Baseline único del gate: persistencia q90 rolling-252 sobre y observable en t (mismo
1020-   cutoff y_{t−5} para ambos). EWMA/HAR/celdas H-RISK: solo descriptivo.
1021-10. Métrica: Δ = pinball_modelo − pinball_persistencia, pareada, AGREGADA POR SEMANA ISO
1022-    del origen (targets solapados ⇒ 250 pérdidas diarias ≠ N=250).
1023-11. IC95 bootstrap circular b=4 semanas, 2000 resamples, seed 42 — fijados aquí.
1024-12. **WIN solo si el borde SUPERIOR del IC95(Δ) < 0.** Todo lo demás = NO_RECHAZA.
1025-13. Smoke test funcional en ≤2023 (correr sin errores); CERO métricas comparativas.
1026-14. Política de fallo: error de inferencia en un origen ⇒ se excluye y loguea; >5% de
1027-    orígenes fallidos ⇒ corrida INVÁLIDA. Versiones de librerías al artefacto.
1028-    Cambiar config tras ver problemas en TEST ⇒ corrida INVÁLIDA.
1029:15. Contabilidad: abrir 2025 = **+1 trial (N 71→72)**. Pierde ⇒ puerta
1030-    transformer/fundacional COP CERRADA por escrito. Gana ⇒ habilita UN trial económico
1031-    posterior (techo de sizing tipo v13), juez = forward post-freeze.

1049-sup < 0). Es, por lejos, lo más cerca que algo ha estado de batir a la persistencia
1050-(el transformer local ni siquiera ganó en media). La media favorece al fundacional con
1051-claridad; la evidencia no alcanza el bar pre-firmado, y el bar no se mueve ex-post.

1058:diagnóstico de hoy solo genera esa hipótesis futura, jamás un re-test sobre 2025.

+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# H-V13-QRISK-01 — probabilistic weekly leverage ceiling (sealed 2026-07-21)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def compute_v13_leverage_ceilings(df):
+    """H-V13-QRISK-01 (pre-registered & SEALED 2026-07-21) — probabilistic weekly leverage
+    ceiling, computed INSIDE the engine so a design-run judges the motor, not an external
+    replay (the previous external instrument was ruled INVALID_INSTRUMENT).
+
+    Sealed formula (zero grid; every constant is a declared ex-ante prior):
+
+      q90_hat_t   = 0.5 * persistence_t + 0.5 * qr_pred_t          (50/50, no fitted weights)
+      ceiling_t   = 1.5 * clip(median_design_t / q90_hat_t, 0.5, 1.0)   -> in [0.75, 1.5]
+
+      persistence_t   Empirical q90 over the trailing 252 daily observations (strictly < t)
+                      of the realized 5d range — the unbeaten persistence baseline of
+                      H-VOLE-01 ("liston imbatido").
+      qr_pred_t       statsmodels QuantReg (tau=0.90) prediction of the weekly realized
+                      range (% of the close preceding the week) from the 4 winning
+                      H-RISK-FAM-01 features {f2 vol-of-vol 20d, f3 Monday gap-tail prob,
+                      f4 EMBI acceleration z, f5 RESINT z}. Feature definitions are
+                      REPLICATED VERBATIM from scripts/analysis/cop_risk_family_screen.py
+                      ::load_weekly() (that script embeds them inside a fixed-seed loader,
+                      so importing is not possible; any edit there must be mirrored here).
+                      The QR is re-fit each week on an EXPANDING window of weeks fully
+                      realized strictly before t (a week's target is known its Friday < t)
+                      — it never sees t or the future.
+      median_design_t Causal expanding median of q90_hat over design weeks
+                      2020-01-01 <= s < min(t, 2025-01-01). Documented one-time choice:
+                      "mediana_diseno sobre 2020-2024, expanding hasta t", capped at the
+                      design end so the constant freezes after 2024.
+
+    One-time documented implementation choices (no variants were tried — quant-constitution
+    §1; if the sealed formula looks improvable, it is REPORTED, never changed here):
+      * "rango 5d realizado" = TRUE 5-day range: (max(high, 5d) - min(low, 5d)) divided by
+        the close preceding the 5d window, in %. Chosen because it is unit-coherent with
+        the QR target range_pct of H-RISK-FAM-01, so the sealed 50/50 blend averages two
+        estimators of the SAME quantity.
+      * QR needs >= 52 realized training weeks and finite features at t; otherwise the
+        fail-safe is q90_hat_t = persistence_t alone (counted in meta, never optimized).
+      * Warm-up: the design median needs >= 26 prior q90_hat values; before that (H1-2020
+        only, outside every design year) the ceiling is inactive (= 1.5). The ceiling can
+        NEVER exceed 1.5 (v13 is a subset of v12 by construction).
+
+    Returns (ceilings, meta): dict {Monday Timestamp (W-SUN period start) -> ceiling float}
+    and a diagnostics dict.
+
+    Data source (part of the sealed definition, documented once): the daily table is built
+    from seeds/latest/usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet — the SAME source cop_risk_family_screen
+    ::load_weekly() validated the features on (history from 2015). The engine's loader df
+    starts 2019-12, which would silently change the conditioning sets (persistence roll-252,
+    f3's 252-Monday tail, QR training depth) versus what H-RISK-FAM-01 screened. The `df`
+    argument is therefore NOT used for the risk table; it is kept for signature stability.
+    """
+    import statsmodels.api as sm
+
+    seed_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "seeds/latest/usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet"
+    daily_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN.parquet"
+    monthly_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/MACRO_MONTHLY_CLEAN.parquet"
+    if not (seed_path.exists() and daily_path.exists() and monthly_path.exists()):
+        # Fail LOUD: a candidate flag silently degrading to v12 behavior would corrupt a
+        # design-run. v13 cannot run without its sealed inputs.
+        raise RuntimeError("v13_ceiling_enabled: seed/MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN/MACRO_MONTHLY_CLEAN missing")
+
+    macro = pd.read_parquet(daily_path)
+    macro.index = pd.to_datetime(macro.index)
+    mcols = {c.upper(): c for c in macro.columns}
+    vix = macro[mcols["VOLT_VIX_USA_D_VIX"]].rename("vix")
+    embi = macro[mcols["CRSK_SPREAD_EMBI_COL_D_EMBI"]].rename("embi")
+    monthly = pd.read_parquet(monthly_path)
+    monthly.index = pd.to_datetime(monthly.index)
+    resint = monthly[[c for c in monthly.columns if "RESINT" in c.upper()][0]].rename("resint")
+
+    d = pd.read_parquet(seed_path)
+    d["date"] = pd.to_datetime(d["time"]).dt.tz_localize(None)
+    d = d[["date", "open", "high", "low", "close"]].dropna(subset=["close"]).copy()
+    d = d.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
+    d["ret"] = np.log(d["close"] / d["close"].shift(1))
+    d["gap"] = (d["open"] / d["close"].shift(1) - 1).abs()
+    d["dow"] = d["date"].dt.dayofweek
+    d["week"] = d["date"].dt.to_period("W-SUN")
+    d["vol20"] = d["ret"].rolling(20).std() * np.sqrt(252)
+    # realized 5d range (true 5-day range, % of the close preceding the window); the value
+    # dated day t uses data up to t only -> taking values with date < monday is causal.
+    d["rng5"] = ((d["high"].rolling(5).max() - d["low"].rolling(5).min())
+                 / d["close"].shift(5) * 100)
+
+    # ---- weekly feature table (verbatim replication of cop_risk_family_screen.load_weekly)
+    rows = []
+    for wk, g in d.groupby("week"):
+        if len(g) < 3:
+            continue
+        week_start_day = g["date"].iloc[0]
+        prev = d[d["date"] < week_start_day]
+        if len(prev) < 260:
+            continue
+        prev_close = prev["close"].iloc[-1]
+        range_pct = (g["high"].max() - g["low"].min()) / prev_close * 100
+        asof = prev["date"].iloc[-1]
+        # f2 vol-of-vol 20d (identical to screen)
+        vv = prev["vol20"].tail(20).diff().std() * np.sqrt(252)
+        # f4 EMBI acceleration z (identical to screen)
+        embi_ser = embi.loc[:asof].dropna()
+        embi_acc = (float((embi_ser.diff(5).iloc[-1] - embi_ser.diff(5).tail(252).mean())
+                          / (embi_ser.diff(5).tail(252).std() + 1e-9))
+                    if len(embi_ser) > 260 else np.nan)
+        # f5 RESINT z with 45d publication lag (identical to screen)
+        res_ser = resint.loc[:asof - pd.Timedelta(days=45)].dropna()
+        res_z = (float((res_ser.iloc[-1] - res_ser.tail(12).mean())
+                       / (res_ser.tail(12).std() + 1e-9))
+                 if len(res_ser) > 12 else np.nan)
+        # f3 Monday gap-tail conditional prob given VIX tercile (identical to screen)
+        vix_t1 = float(vix.loc[:asof].iloc[-1]) if len(vix.loc[:asof]) else np.nan
+        prev_mon = prev[prev["dow"] == 0].tail(252)
+        if len(prev_mon) > 30 and not np.isnan(vix_t1):
+            vix_al = vix.reindex(prev_mon["date"], method="ffill")
+            ter = pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, labels=False, duplicates="drop")
+            my_ter = 1
+            if len(pd.unique(ter.dropna())) == 3:
+                _cut = pd.cut([vix_t1], bins=pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, retbins=True,
+                              duplicates="drop")[1], labels=False, include_lowest=True)[0]
+                if pd.isna(_cut):
+                    _cut = 0 if vix_t1 < float(vix_al.min()) else 2
+                my_ter = int(_cut)
+            mask = (ter == my_ter).to_numpy()
+            thr = prev_mon["gap"].quantile(0.90)
+            f3 = float((prev_mon["gap"].to_numpy()[mask] > thr).mean()) if mask.sum() > 5 else np.nan
+        else:
+            f3 = np.nan
+        # persistence: q90 of the trailing 252 realized-5d-range values strictly < t
+        rng5_hist = prev["rng5"].dropna().tail(252)
+        pers = float(rng5_hist.quantile(0.90)) if len(rng5_hist) >= 252 else np.nan
+        rows.append({"monday": wk.start_time, "last_date": g["date"].iloc[-1],
+                     "range_pct": float(range_pct), "persistence": pers,
+                     "f2": float(vv) if np.isfinite(vv) else np.nan,
+                     "f3": f3, "f4": embi_acc, "f5": res_z})
+    W = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("monday").reset_index(drop=True)
+
+    feat_cols = ["f2", "f3", "f4", "f5"]
+    design_start = pd.Timestamp("2020-01-01")
+    design_end = pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01")
+    ceilings, q90_hist = {}, []          # q90_hist: (monday, q90_hat) for the causal median
+    n_qr_fallback = n_inactive = n_weeks = 0
+    for i in range(len(W)):
+        t = W["monday"].iloc[i]
+        if t < design_start:
+            continue
+        n_weeks += 1
+        row_feats = W[feat_cols].iloc[i].to_numpy(dtype=float)
+        train = W[(W["last_date"] < t) & W["range_pct"].notna()].dropna(subset=feat_cols)
+        qr_pred = np.nan
+        if np.isfinite(row_feats).all() and len(train) >= 52:
+            try:
+                X = sm.add_constant(train[feat_cols].to_numpy(dtype=float))
+                qr = sm.QuantReg(train["range_pct"].to_numpy(dtype=float), X).fit(q=0.90)
+                qr_pred = float(np.asarray(qr.params) @ np.concatenate(([1.0], row_feats)))
+            except Exception:
+                qr_pred = np.nan
+        pers = W["persistence"].iloc[i]
+        if np.isfinite(pers) and np.isfinite(qr_pred):
+            q90_hat = 0.5 * pers + 0.5 * qr_pred
+        elif np.isfinite(pers):
+            q90_hat = float(pers)
+            n_qr_fallback += 1
+        else:
+            q90_hat = np.nan
+        # causal design median: q90_hat values with design_start <= s < min(t, design_end)
+        med_vals = [v for (s, v) in q90_hist if s < min(t, design_end) and np.isfinite(v)]
+        if np.isfinite(q90_hat):
+            q90_hist.append((t, q90_hat))
+        if len(med_vals) >= 26 and np.isfinite(q90_hat) and q90_hat > 0:
+            med = float(np.median(med_vals))
+            ceilings[t] = 1.5 * float(np.clip(med / q90_hat, 0.5, 1.0))
+        else:
+            ceilings[t] = 1.5   # warm-up / fail-safe: inactive, never raises above 1.5
+            n_inactive += 1
+    meta = {
+        "n_weeks": n_weeks, "n_qr_fallback": n_qr_fallback, "n_ceiling_inactive": n_inactive,
+        "median_design_final": (float(np.median([v for (s, v) in q90_hist
+                                                 if s < design_end and np.isfinite(v)]))
+                                if q90_hist else None),
+        "ceiling_mean": float(np.mean(list(ceilings.values()))) if ceilings else None,
+        "ceiling_min": float(np.min(list(ceilings.values()))) if ceilings else None,
+    }
+    return ceilings, meta
+
+

## H-V13-QRISK-01 (PRE-REGISTRO 2026-07-21, 0 miradas) — techo de leverage probabilístico

**Fórmula SELLADA (cero grid; cada constante con su prior declarado):**
- `q90_hat_t` = 0.5·persistencia(q90 rolling-252 del rango 5d) + 0.5·QR(q90) sobre las
  4 features ganadoras de H-RISK-FAM-01 {vol-of-vol, gap-cola, EMBI-acel, RESINT},
  ajustada walk-forward SOLO con datos < t (composición 50/50: el listón imbatido ancla,
  las features validadas modulan — sin pesos optimizados).
- `techo_t = 1.5 × clip(mediana_diseño(q90_hat) / q90_hat_t, 0.5, 1.0)` — riesgo predicho
  sobre la mediana ⇒ el techo baja proporcionalmente; JAMÁS sube de 1.5 (v13 ⊂ v12).
- Todo lo demás idéntico a v12. Ventanas canónicas del operador (diseño 2020/22-24).
- **Design-run pareado** (v12 vs v13, mismas señales): abre +1 trial (N 66→67).
  Aprobación por diseño = Calmar_v13 ≥ Calmar_v12 en 2022-2024 (replay de salidas con
  effective-HS recalculado por leverage). **Juez real = forward** desde su freeze,
  mismo protocolo sellado de v12 (reloj propio, corte-52).

## H-V13-QRISK-01 design-run: INSTRUMENTO INVÁLIDO (2026-07-21) · trials 66→67

El replay de salidas construido para el design-run pareado NO reproduce al motor: dio
v12 = −12.8% en 2022-24 donde el motor real (autoridad, `cop_v12_design/`) dio +12.1%
compuesto — la aproximación effective-HS/producción-wrapper es incorrecta. **Regla
aplicada: un instrumento que no reproduce al motor no puede juzgar nada.** La mirada se
contabiliza igual (+1, conservador), el resultado se marca INVALID_INSTRUMENT y NO se
interpreta ni a favor ni en contra de v13.

**Pendiente**: el design-run válido de v13 exige implementar el techo dinámico DENTRO
del motor (flag de candidata, mismo camino que validó a v12). El spec de v13 sigue
sellado sin cambios. v12 queda formalmente congelada para el lunes:
`config/execution/smart_simple_v12_lev_cap.yaml` + `config/strategy_manifests/usdcop_v12.yaml`
(hash 3bc28b36448892a1).

## RE-MEDICIÓN #4 (2026-07-21) — el diseño 2021-2024 con el motor HONESTO es NEGATIVO

Corridas pareadas v11/v12 con el motor actual (purga + fills open-aware) sobre las
ventanas canónicas — los "+12.1% vs +2.4%" del diseño eran del motor PRE-purga y quedan
retractados como evidencia (la fuga inflaba precisamente los años de diseño):

| Año | v11 (cap 2.0) | v12 (cap 1.5) |
|---|---|---|
| 2021 | −2.53% / DD 4.2 | −2.14% / DD 3.8 |
| 2022 | −9.05% / DD 9.4 | −9.22% / DD 9.4 |
| 2023 | −2.54% / DD 6.7 | −2.53% / DD 6.9 |
| 2024 | −4.19% / DD 8.0 | **+4.60% / DD 3.5** |
| **Compuesto 21-24** | **−17.22%** | **−9.43%** |
| 2025 (celda pagada) | +7.35% / DD 7.8 | +7.69% / DD 5.7 |

**Lecturas obligadas:**
1. **La familia smart_simple NO tiene rentabilidad demostrada en NINGÚN período limpio de
   diseño** — toda la rentabilidad histórica vive en 2025, el año sobre el que el grid de
   42 celdas seleccionó. Es la confirmación más fuerte hasta ahora de que el backtest
   2025 es artefacto de selección (coherente con DSR 0.72<0.95 y NULL-B).
2. v12 domina a v11 en riesgo en todos los años (DD menor o igual; 2024 positivo; 2025
   Calmar ~1.36 vs ~0.94) — el cap sigue siendo mejor mecánica — pero "mejor que v11"
   ya no significa "rentable en diseño".
3. **El único período positivo limpio es el forward 2026 (+3.36% YTD)** — el juez real.
   El Corte A (16-sep) y W6 (si NULL-A ≥ v11, la mecánica sin modelo ES la estrategia)
   pasan a ser el evento central del track.
4. v12 va a paper el lunes igual (costo cero, menos riesgo que v11); su caso de diseño
   se re-basa en dominancia de riesgo, no en retorno. Expectativas re-fijadas por escrito.
0 trials nuevos (re-medición de celdas pagadas con mejor instrumento).

El replay de salidas construido para el design-run pareado NO reproduce al motor: dio
v12 = −12.8% en 2022-24 donde el motor real (autoridad, `cop_v12_design/`) dio +12.1%

interpreta ni a favor ni en contra de v13.

**Pendiente**: el design-run válido de v13 exige implementar el techo dinámico DENTRO
del motor (flag de candidata, mismo camino que validó a v12). El spec de v13 sigue
sellado sin cambios. v12 queda formalmente congelada para el lunes:
`config/execution/smart_simple_v12_lev_cap.yaml` + `config/strategy_manifests/usdcop_v12.yaml`
(hash 3bc28b36448892a1).

## RE-MEDICIÓN #4 (2026-07-21) — el diseño 2021-2024 con el motor HONESTO es NEGATIVO

Corridas pareadas v11/v12 con el motor actual (purga + fills open-aware) sobre las
ventanas canónicas — los "+12.1% vs +2.4%" del diseño eran del motor PRE-purga y quedan
retractados como evidencia (la fuga inflaba precisamente los años de diseño):

| Año | v11 (cap 2.0) | v12 (cap 1.5) |
|---|---|---|
| 2021 | −2.53% / DD 4.2 | −2.14% / DD 3.8 |
| 2022 | −9.05% / DD 9.4 | −9.22% / DD 9.4 |
| 2023 | −2.54% / DD 6.7 | −2.53% / DD 6.9 |
| 2024 | −4.19% / DD 8.0 | **+4.60% / DD 3.5** |
| **Compuesto 21-24** | **−17.22%** | **−9.43%** |
| 2025 (celda pagada) | +7.35% / DD 7.8 | +7.69% / DD 5.7 |

**Lecturas obligadas:**
1. **La familia smart_simple NO tiene rentabilidad demostrada en NINGÚN período limpio de
   diseño** — toda la rentabilidad histórica vive en 2025, el año sobre el que el grid de
   42 celdas seleccionó. Es la confirmación más fuerte hasta ahora de que el backtest
   2025 es artefacto de selección (coherente con DSR 0.72<0.95 y NULL-B).
2. v12 domina a v11 en riesgo en todos los años (DD menor o igual; 2024 positivo; 2025
   Calmar ~1.36 vs ~0.94) — el cap sigue siendo mejor mecánica — pero "mejor que v11"
   ya no significa "rentable en diseño".
3. **El único período positivo limpio es el forward 2026 (+3.36% YTD)** — el juez real.
   El Corte A (16-sep) y W6 (si NULL-A ≥ v11, la mecánica sin modelo ES la estrategia)
   pasan a ser el evento central del track.
4. v12 va a paper el lunes igual (costo cero, menos riesgo que v11); su caso de diseño
   se re-basa en dominancia de riesgo, no en retorno. Expectativas re-fijadas por escrito.
0 trials nuevos (re-medición de celdas pagadas con mejor instrumento).

## ORÁCULO 2025/2026 (descriptivo, 0 trials) + H-TP-LADDER-01 (PLANNED, 0 miradas)

**Techo de la clase de mecánica** (M5 real, entrada solo lunes-cierre como v11, salida
perfecta, lev 1.5, costos 1bp/lado — `.claude/evidence/cop_oracle/2026-07-21/`):

| Año | Oráculo libre | Oráculo clase-v11 | Realizado v11 | Captura |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2025 | 179pp | **120pp** | +7.35% | **6.1%** |
| 2026 YTD | 103pp | 50pp | +3.36% | 6.8% |

**Lecturas:**
1. **El 20-30% SÍ existe dentro de la clase de mecánica** (bastaría capturar 17-25% del
   techo vs el 6% actual) — la meta es geométricamente alcanzable sin cambiar la clase
   de entrada. La restricción no es la señal: es CUÁNTO se deja correr al ganador.
2. Los bolsillos grandes (top semanas 3.7-6.0pp disponibles) mueren hoy en el TP fijo
   ~1.6%: el TP captura la cola corta de semanas que ofrecían 3× más.
3. **PROHIBICIÓN explícita** (constitución §1 + evento de hoy): entrenar CUALQUIER modelo
   por prueba-y-error contra estos puntos de entrada/salida 2025 = imitación del oráculo
   = la forma pura del overfitting que hoy vimos colapsar el diseño a −17%. El oráculo
   es mapa de techo, jamás target de entrenamiento.

**H-TP-LADDER-01 (PLANNED, la hipótesis mecánica que el diagnóstico habilita)**: salida
escalonada pre-declarada — 50% del tamaño al TP actual (vol-escalado ~1.6%), 50% restante
al 2×TP o week-end, mismo HS. UNA variable (estructura de salida), prior ex-ante (el
oráculo muestra bolsillos 2-3× el TP; el skill em-fx: dejar correr en EM con stop de
tamaño), diseño en motor 2021-2024 pareado vs v12 + re-medición 2025 de la misma celda,
juez = forward. Costo: +1 trial al abrir. Nota: revisa el DO-NOT histórico "no trailing"
— esto NO es trailing (es ladder fijo); si el operador la aprueba, es la siguiente en cola.

    1: """H-RISK-FAM-01 â€” screening de la familia de riesgo pre-registrada (ABRE resultados: +5 trials).

   10:   semanal (max high - min low, en % del cierre previo).

   14:   q90 = 1.645 * EWMA-vol * sqrt(5), lambda 0.94). Win = IC95 block-bootstrap (b=4) de
   15:   la DIFERENCIA de perdida excluye 0 a favor de la celda.
   16: - Se publica la tabla ENTERA. Consecuencia pre-firmada: >=1 win -> 1 trial economico

   45:     "2020-01-29","2020-03-03","2020-03-15","2020-04-29","2020-06-10","2020-07-29",
   46:     "2020-09-16","2020-11-05","2020-12-16","2021-01-27","2021-03-17","2021-04-28",

   54: def load_weekly():
   55:     d = pd.read_parquet(REPO / "seeds/latest/usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet")
   56:     d["time"] = pd.to_datetime(d["time"]).dt.tz_localize(None)
   57:     d = d[d["time"] <= END_DESIGN].sort_values("time").reset_index(drop=True)
   58:     d["ret"] = np.log(d["close"] / d["close"].shift(1))
   59:     d["gap"] = (d["open"] / d["close"].shift(1) - 1).abs()
   60:     d["dow"] = d["time"].dt.dayofweek
   61:     d["week"] = d["time"].dt.to_period("W-SUN")
   62: 
   63:     macro = pd.read_parquet(REPO / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN.parquet")
   64:     macro.index = pd.to_datetime(macro.index)
   65:     cols = {c.upper(): c for c in macro.columns}
   66:     vix = macro[cols["VOLT_VIX_USA_D_VIX"]].rename("vix")
   67:     embi = macro[cols["CRSK_SPREAD_EMBI_COL_D_EMBI"]].rename("embi")
   68: 
   69:     monthly = pd.read_parquet(REPO / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/MACRO_MONTHLY_CLEAN.parquet")
   70:     monthly.index = pd.to_datetime(monthly.index)
   71:     resint = monthly[[c for c in monthly.columns if "RESINT" in c.upper()][0]].rename("resint")
   72: 
   73:     rows = []
   74:     vol20 = d["ret"].rolling(20).std() * np.sqrt(252)
   75:     d["vol20"] = vol20
   76:     for wk, g in d.groupby("week"):
   77:         if len(g) < 3:
   78:             continue
   79:         monday = g.iloc[0]
   80:         prev = d[d["time"] < g["time"].iloc[0]]
   81:         if len(prev) < 260:
   82:             continue
   83:         prev_close = prev["close"].iloc[-1]
   84:         rng_pct = (g["high"].max() - g["low"].min()) / prev_close * 100
   85:         gap_bps = monday["gap"] * 1e4
   86:         p90_gap = prev.loc[prev["dow"] == 0, "gap"].tail(252).quantile(0.90) * 1e4
   87:         asof = prev["time"].iloc[-1]
   88:         # features t-1 (todas conocibles el viernes previo)
   89:         vv = prev["vol20"].tail(20).diff().std() * np.sqrt(252)          # F2 vol-of-vol
   90:         vix_t1 = float(vix.loc[:asof].iloc[-1]) if len(vix.loc[:asof]) else np.nan
   91:         embi_ser = embi.loc[:asof].dropna()
   92:         embi_acc = float((embi_ser.diff(5).iloc[-1] - embi_ser.diff(5).tail(252).mean())
   93:                          / (embi_ser.diff(5).tail(252).std() + 1e-9)) if len(embi_ser) > 260 else np.nan
   94:         res_ser = resint.loc[:asof - pd.Timedelta(days=45)].dropna()     # lag publicacion
   95:         res_z = float((res_ser.iloc[-1] - res_ser.tail(12).mean())
   96:                       / (res_ser.tail(12).std() + 1e-9)) if len(res_ser) > 12 else np.nan
   97:         # F3: prob condicional de gap-cola dado nivel vix (kernel binario simple en terciles)
   98:         prev_mon = prev[prev["dow"] == 0].tail(252)
   99:         if len(prev_mon) > 30 and not np.isnan(vix_t1):
  100:             vix_al = vix.reindex(prev_mon["time"], method="ffill")
  101:             ter = pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, labels=False, duplicates="drop")
  102:             my_ter = 1
  103:             if len(pd.unique(ter.dropna())) == 3:
  104:                 _cut = pd.cut([vix_t1], bins=pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, retbins=True,
  105:                               duplicates="drop")[1], labels=False, include_lowest=True)[0]
  106:                 # vix_t1 fuera del rango historico -> clip al tercil extremo
  107:                 if pd.isna(_cut):
  108:                     _cut = 0 if vix_t1 < float(vix_al.min()) else 2
  109:                 my_ter = int(_cut)
  110:             mask = (ter == my_ter).to_numpy()
  111:             thr = prev_mon["gap"].quantile(0.90)
  112:             f3 = float((prev_mon["gap"].to_numpy()[mask] > thr).mean()) if mask.sum() > 5 else np.nan
  113:         else:
  114:             f3 = np.nan
  115:         # F1 calendario: evento en la semana operada (conocible ex-ante)
  116:         wk_start, wk_end = g["time"].iloc[0], g["time"].iloc[-1]
  117:         fomc_wk = int(((FOMC >= wk_start) & (FOMC <= wk_end)).any())
  118:         last_bday = pd.date_range(wk_start.replace(day=1),
  119:                                   wk_start + pd.offsets.MonthEnd(0), freq="B")[-1]
  120:         banrep_wk = int(wk_start <= last_bday <= wk_end)
  121:         ipc_wk = int(any((t.day in (4, 5, 6, 7, 8)) for t in g["time"]))
  122:         f1 = int(fomc_wk or banrep_wk or ipc_wk)
  123:         # EWMA vol para el baseline de pinball
  124:         _ew = ewma_volatility(prev["ret"].dropna().to_numpy()[-260:],
  125:                               lam=0.94, periods_per_year=252)
  126:         sig_ewma = float(np.asarray(_ew).ravel()[-1])   # la serie EWMA: tomar el ultimo valor
  127:         rows.append({"monday": monday["time"], "gap_bps": gap_bps,
  128:                      "gap_week": int(gap_bps > p90_gap), "range_pct": rng_pct,
  129:                      "f1_event": f1, "f2_volofvol": vv, "f3_gaptail": f3,
  130:                      "f4_embiacc": embi_acc, "f5_resintz": res_z,
  131:                      "base_rate": float(prev.loc[prev["dow"] == 0, "gap"].tail(252)
  132:                                         .gt(prev.loc[prev["dow"] == 0, "gap"].tail(252)
  133:                                             .quantile(0.90)).mean()),
  134:                      "sigma_ewma": sig_ewma})
  135:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
  136: 
  137: 
  138: def purged_kfold_idx(n, k=5, purge=1):
  139:     fold = np.array_split(np.arange(n), k)
  140:     for i in range(k):
  141:         test = fold[i]
  142:         tr = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), test)
  143:         tr = tr[(tr < test.min() - purge) | (tr > test.max() + purge)]
  144:         yield tr, test
  145: 
  146: 
  147: def screen():
  148:     from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression, QuantileRegressor
  149:     W = load_weekly()
  150:     print(f"semanas de disenio: {len(W)} ({W.monday.min().date()} -> {W.monday.max().date()})")
  151:     print(f"tasa base gap_week: {W.gap_week.mean():.3f}")
  152:     cells = ["f1_event", "f2_volofvol", "f3_gaptail", "f4_embiacc", "f5_resintz"]
  153:     results = {}
  154:     for cell in cells:
  155:         d = W.dropna(subset=[cell, "gap_week", "range_pct", "sigma_ewma"]).reset_index(drop=True)
  156:         n = len(d)
  157:         x = d[cell].to_numpy().reshape(-1, 1)
  158:         # --- Brier: modelo univariado vs frecuencia base rolling ---
  159:         p_model = np.full(n, np.nan)
  160:         for tr, te in purged_kfold_idx(n):
  161:             if len(np.unique(d.gap_week.iloc[tr])) < 2:
  162:                 p_model[te] = d.gap_week.iloc[tr].mean()
  163:                 continue
  164:             m = LogisticRegression().fit(x[tr], d.gap_week.iloc[tr])
  165:             p_model[te] = m.predict_proba(x[te])[:, 1]
  166:         brier_m = (p_model - d.gap_week) ** 2
  167:         brier_b = (d.base_rate - d.gap_week) ** 2
  168:         db = circular_block_bootstrap((brier_b - brier_m).to_numpy(), np.mean, block=4)
  169:         # --- pinball q90 del rango vs baseline persistencia EWMA ---
  170:         q_model = np.full(n, np.nan)
  171:         for tr, te in purged_kfold_idx(n):
  172:             qr = QuantileRegressor(quantile=0.90, alpha=1e-4, solver="highs").fit(
  173:                 x[tr], d.range_pct.iloc[tr])
  174:             q_model[te] = qr.predict(x[te])
  175:         q_base = 1.645 * d.sigma_ewma / np.sqrt(52) * np.sqrt(5) / np.sqrt(5) * 100 * np.sqrt(5) / np.sqrt(252/5)
  176:         # baseline correcto: q90 semanal = 1.645 * sigma_ewma_anual * sqrt(5/252) * 100
  177:         q_base = 1.645 * d.sigma_ewma * np.sqrt(5 / 252) * 100
  178:         def pinball(y, q, tau=0.90):
  179:             e = y - q
  180:             return np.maximum(tau * e, (tau - 1) * e)
  181:         pin_m = pinball(d.range_pct.to_numpy(), q_model)
  182:         pin_b = pinball(d.range_pct.to_numpy(), q_base.to_numpy())
  183:         # NULL JUSTO (correccion contra nosotros mismos, 2026-07-21): el baseline
  184:         # 1.645*sigma*sqrt(5) es el q90 de un RETORNO, no del RANGO -> subestima y
  185:         # cualquier modelo gana por el intercepto. El null que aisla la FEATURE es
  186:         # intercepto-solo en el MISMO purged CV.
  187:         q_null = np.full(n, np.nan)
  188:         for tr, te in purged_kfold_idx(n):
  189:             q_null[te] = np.quantile(d.range_pct.iloc[tr], 0.90)
  190:         pin_null = pinball(d.range_pct.to_numpy(), q_null)
  191:         dp = circular_block_bootstrap(pin_null - pin_m, np.mean, block=4)
  192:         win_brier = db["ci95"][0] is not None and db["ci95"][0] > 0
  193:         win_pin = dp["ci95"][0] is not None and dp["ci95"][0] > 0
  194:         results[cell] = {
  195:             "n": n,
  196:             "brier_model": float(np.nanmean(brier_m)), "brier_base": float(np.nanmean(brier_b)),
  197:             "delta_brier_ci95": db["ci95"], "WIN_brier": bool(win_brier),
  198:             "pinball_model": float(np.nanmean(pin_m)), "pinball_base_ewma": float(np.nanmean(pin_b)),
  199:             "pinball_null_intercepto": float(np.nanmean(pin_null)),
  200:             "delta_pinball_vs_null_ci95": dp["ci95"], "WIN_pinball": bool(win_pin),
  201:         }
  202:         print(f"{cell}: n={n} | Brier {np.nanmean(brier_m):.4f} vs base "
  203:               f"{np.nanmean(brier_b):.4f} (WIN={win_brier}) | pinball "
  204:               f"{np.nanmean(pin_m):.3f} vs NULL-intercepto {np.nanmean(pin_null):.3f} "
  205:               f"(ewma mal-especificado: {np.nanmean(pin_b):.3f}) (WIN={win_pin})")
  206:     wins = [c for c, r in results.items() if r["WIN_brier"] or r["WIN_pinball"]]
  207:     verdict = {"family": "H-RISK-FAM-01", "cells": results, "wins": wins,
  208:                "trials_spent": 5, "consequence": (
  209:                    "1 trial economico adicional (sizing P(gap), v13, juez forward)"
  210:                    if wins else "familia CERRADA por escrito")}
  211:     o = REPO / ".claude/evidence/cop_risk_family" / date.today().isoformat()
  212:     o.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  213:     (o / "screen_results.json").write_text(json.dumps(verdict, indent=2, default=str))
  214:     shutil.copy(__file__, o / "generator_script.py")
  215:     print(f"\nWINS: {wins if wins else 'NINGUNA â€” familia cerrada'}")
  216:     print(f"artefacto -> {o}")
  217:     return verdict
  218: 
  219: 
  220: if __name__ == "__main__":
  221:     screen()
  253: def compute_v13_leverage_ceilings(df):
  254:     """H-V13-QRISK-01 (pre-registered & SEALED 2026-07-21) â€” probabilistic weekly leverage
  255:     ceiling, computed INSIDE the engine so a design-run judges the motor, not an external
  256:     replay (the previous external instrument was ruled INVALID_INSTRUMENT).
  257: 
  258:     Sealed formula (zero grid; every constant is a declared ex-ante prior):
  259: 
  260:       q90_hat_t   = 0.5 * persistence_t + 0.5 * qr_pred_t          (50/50, no fitted weights)
  261:       ceiling_t   = 1.5 * clip(median_design_t / q90_hat_t, 0.5, 1.0)   -> in [0.75, 1.5]
  262: 
  263:       persistence_t   Empirical q90 over the trailing 252 daily observations (strictly < t)
  264:                       of the realized 5d range â€” the unbeaten persistence baseline of
  265:                       H-VOLE-01 ("liston imbatido").
  266:       qr_pred_t       statsmodels QuantReg (tau=0.90) prediction of the weekly realized
  267:                       range (% of the close preceding the week) from the 4 winning
  268:                       H-RISK-FAM-01 features {f2 vol-of-vol 20d, f3 Monday gap-tail prob,
  269:                       f4 EMBI acceleration z, f5 RESINT z}. Feature definitions are
  270:                       REPLICATED VERBATIM from scripts/analysis/cop_risk_family_screen.py
  271:                       ::load_weekly() (that script embeds them inside a fixed-seed loader,
  272:                       so importing is not possible; any edit there must be mirrored here).
  273:                       The QR is re-fit each week on an EXPANDING window of weeks fully
  274:                       realized strictly before t (a week's target is known its Friday < t)
  275:                       â€” it never sees t or the future.
  276:       median_design_t Causal expanding median of q90_hat over design weeks
  277:                       2020-01-01 <= s < min(t, 2025-01-01). Documented one-time choice:
  278:                       "mediana_diseno sobre 2020-2024, expanding hasta t", capped at the
  279:                       design end so the constant freezes after 2024.
  280: 
  281:     One-time documented implementation choices (no variants were tried â€” quant-constitution
  282:     Â§1; if the sealed formula looks improvable, it is REPORTED, never changed here):
  283:       * "rango 5d realizado" = TRUE 5-day range: (max(high, 5d) - min(low, 5d)) divided by
  284:         the close preceding the 5d window, in %. Chosen because it is unit-coherent with
  285:         the QR target range_pct of H-RISK-FAM-01, so the sealed 50/50 blend averages two
  286:         estimators of the SAME quantity.
  287:       * QR needs >= 52 realized training weeks and finite features at t; otherwise the
  288:         fail-safe is q90_hat_t = persistence_t alone (counted in meta, never optimized).
  289:       * Warm-up: the design median needs >= 26 prior q90_hat values; before that (H1-2020
  290:         only, outside every design year) the ceiling is inactive (= 1.5). The ceiling can
  291:         NEVER exceed 1.5 (v13 is a subset of v12 by construction).
  292: 
  293:     Returns (ceilings, meta): dict {Monday Timestamp (W-SUN period start) -> ceiling float}
  294:     and a diagnostics dict.
  295: 
  296:     Data source (part of the sealed definition, documented once): the daily table is built
  297:     from seeds/latest/usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet â€” the SAME source cop_risk_family_screen
  298:     ::load_weekly() validated the features on (history from 2015). The engine's loader df
  299:     starts 2019-12, which would silently change the conditioning sets (persistence roll-252,
  300:     f3's 252-Monday tail, QR training depth) versus what H-RISK-FAM-01 screened. The `df`
  301:     argument is therefore NOT used for the risk table; it is kept for signature stability.
  302:     """
  303:     import statsmodels.api as sm
  304: 
  305:     seed_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "seeds/latest/usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet"
  306:     daily_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN.parquet"
  307:     monthly_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/MACRO_MONTHLY_CLEAN.parquet"
  308:     if not (seed_path.exists() and daily_path.exists() and monthly_path.exists()):
  309:         # Fail LOUD: a candidate flag silently degrading to v12 behavior would corrupt a
  310:         # design-run. v13 cannot run without its sealed inputs.
  311:         raise RuntimeError("v13_ceiling_enabled: seed/MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN/MACRO_MONTHLY_CLEAN missing")
  312: 
  313:     macro = pd.read_parquet(daily_path)
  314:     macro.index = pd.to_datetime(macro.index)
  315:     mcols = {c.upper(): c for c in macro.columns}
  316:     vix = macro[mcols["VOLT_VIX_USA_D_VIX"]].rename("vix")
  317:     embi = macro[mcols["CRSK_SPREAD_EMBI_COL_D_EMBI"]].rename("embi")
  318:     monthly = pd.read_parquet(monthly_path)
  319:     monthly.index = pd.to_datetime(monthly.index)
  320:     resint = monthly[[c for c in monthly.columns if "RESINT" in c.upper()][0]].rename("resint")
  321: 
  322:     d = pd.read_parquet(seed_path)
  323:     d["date"] = pd.to_datetime(d["time"]).dt.tz_localize(None)
  324:     d = d[["date", "open", "high", "low", "close"]].dropna(subset=["close"]).copy()
  325:     d = d.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
  326:     d["ret"] = np.log(d["close"] / d["close"].shift(1))
  327:     d["gap"] = (d["open"] / d["close"].shift(1) - 1).abs()
  328:     d["dow"] = d["date"].dt.dayofweek
  329:     d["week"] = d["date"].dt.to_period("W-SUN")
  330:     d["vol20"] = d["ret"].rolling(20).std() * np.sqrt(252)
  331:     # realized 5d range (true 5-day range, % of the close preceding the window); the value
  332:     # dated day t uses data up to t only -> taking values with date < monday is causal.
  333:     d["rng5"] = ((d["high"].rolling(5).max() - d["low"].rolling(5).min())
  334:                  / d["close"].shift(5) * 100)
  335: 
  336:     # ---- weekly feature table (verbatim replication of cop_risk_family_screen.load_weekly)
  337:     rows = []
  338:     for wk, g in d.groupby("week"):
  339:         if len(g) < 3:
  340:             continue
  341:         week_start_day = g["date"].iloc[0]
  342:         prev = d[d["date"] < week_start_day]
  343:         if len(prev) < 260:
  344:             continue
  345:         prev_close = prev["close"].iloc[-1]
  346:         range_pct = (g["high"].max() - g["low"].min()) / prev_close * 100
  347:         asof = prev["date"].iloc[-1]
  348:         # f2 vol-of-vol 20d (identical to screen)
  349:         vv = prev["vol20"].tail(20).diff().std() * np.sqrt(252)
  350:         # f4 EMBI acceleration z (identical to screen)
  351:         embi_ser = embi.loc[:asof].dropna()
  352:         embi_acc = (float((embi_ser.diff(5).iloc[-1] - embi_ser.diff(5).tail(252).mean())
  353:                           / (embi_ser.diff(5).tail(252).std() + 1e-9))
  354:                     if len(embi_ser) > 260 else np.nan)
  355:         # f5 RESINT z with 45d publication lag (identical to screen)
  356:         res_ser = resint.loc[:asof - pd.Timedelta(days=45)].dropna()
  357:         res_z = (float((res_ser.iloc[-1] - res_ser.tail(12).mean())
  358:                        / (res_ser.tail(12).std() + 1e-9))
  359:                  if len(res_ser) > 12 else np.nan)
  360:         # f3 Monday gap-tail conditional prob given VIX tercile (identical to screen)
  361:         vix_t1 = float(vix.loc[:asof].iloc[-1]) if len(vix.loc[:asof]) else np.nan
  362:         prev_mon = prev[prev["dow"] == 0].tail(252)
  363:         if len(prev_mon) > 30 and not np.isnan(vix_t1):
  364:             vix_al = vix.reindex(prev_mon["date"], method="ffill")
  365:             ter = pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, labels=False, duplicates="drop")
  366:             my_ter = 1
  367:             if len(pd.unique(ter.dropna())) == 3:
  368:                 _cut = pd.cut([vix_t1], bins=pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, retbins=True,
  369:                               duplicates="drop")[1], labels=False, include_lowest=True)[0]
  370:                 if pd.isna(_cut):
  371:                     _cut = 0 if vix_t1 < float(vix_al.min()) else 2
  372:                 my_ter = int(_cut)
  373:             mask = (ter == my_ter).to_numpy()
  374:             thr = prev_mon["gap"].quantile(0.90)
  375:             f3 = float((prev_mon["gap"].to_numpy()[mask] > thr).mean()) if mask.sum() > 5 else np.nan
  376:         else:
  377:             f3 = np.nan
  378:         # persistence: q90 of the trailing 252 realized-5d-range values strictly < t
  379:         rng5_hist = prev["rng5"].dropna().tail(252)
  380:         pers = float(rng5_hist.quantile(0.90)) if len(rng5_hist) >= 252 else np.nan
  381:         rows.append({"monday": wk.start_time, "last_date": g["date"].iloc[-1],
  382:                      "range_pct": float(range_pct), "persistence": pers,
  383:                      "f2": float(vv) if np.isfinite(vv) else np.nan,
  384:                      "f3": f3, "f4": embi_acc, "f5": res_z})
  385:     W = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("monday").reset_index(drop=True)
  386: 
  387:     feat_cols = ["f2", "f3", "f4", "f5"]
  388:     design_start = pd.Timestamp("2020-01-01")
  389:     design_end = pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01")
  390:     ceilings, q90_hist = {}, []          # q90_hist: (monday, q90_hat) for the causal median
  391:     n_qr_fallback = n_inactive = n_weeks = 0
  392:     for i in range(len(W)):
  393:         t = W["monday"].iloc[i]
  394:         if t < design_start:
  395:             continue
  396:         n_weeks += 1
  397:         row_feats = W[feat_cols].iloc[i].to_numpy(dtype=float)
  398:         train = W[(W["last_date"] < t) & W["range_pct"].notna()].dropna(subset=feat_cols)
  399:         qr_pred = np.nan
  400:         if np.isfinite(row_feats).all() and len(train) >= 52:
  401:             try:
  402:                 X = sm.add_constant(train[feat_cols].to_numpy(dtype=float))
  403:                 qr = sm.QuantReg(train["range_pct"].to_numpy(dtype=float), X).fit(q=0.90)
  404:                 qr_pred = float(np.asarray(qr.params) @ np.concatenate(([1.0], row_feats)))
  405:             except Exception:
  406:                 qr_pred = np.nan
  407:         pers = W["persistence"].iloc[i]
  408:         if np.isfinite(pers) and np.isfinite(qr_pred):
  409:             q90_hat = 0.5 * pers + 0.5 * qr_pred
  410:         elif np.isfinite(pers):
  411:             q90_hat = float(pers)
  412:             n_qr_fallback += 1
  413:         else:
  414:             q90_hat = np.nan
  415:         # causal design median: q90_hat values with design_start <= s < min(t, design_end)
  416:         med_vals = [v for (s, v) in q90_hist if s < min(t, design_end) and np.isfinite(v)]
  417:         if np.isfinite(q90_hat):
  418:             q90_hist.append((t, q90_hat))
  419:         if len(med_vals) >= 26 and np.isfinite(q90_hat) and q90_hat > 0:
  420:             med = float(np.median(med_vals))
  421:             ceilings[t] = 1.5 * float(np.clip(med / q90_hat, 0.5, 1.0))
  422:         else:
  423:             ceilings[t] = 1.5   # warm-up / fail-safe: inactive, never raises above 1.5
  424:             n_inactive += 1
  425:     meta = {
  426:         "n_weeks": n_weeks, "n_qr_fallback": n_qr_fallback, "n_ceiling_inactive": n_inactive,
  427:         "median_design_final": (float(np.median([v for (s, v) in q90_hist
  428:                                                  if s < design_end and np.isfinite(v)]))
  429:                                 if q90_hist else None),
  430:         "ceiling_mean": float(np.mean(list(ceilings.values()))) if ceilings else None,
  431:         "ceiling_min": float(np.min(list(ceilings.values()))) if ceilings else None,
  432:     }
  433:     return ceilings, meta
  434: 
  435: 
  440: def _run_weekly_loop(df, feature_cols, cfg, year, collect_week_data=False):
  441:     """
  442:     Smart Simple v3.0 core loop â€” Momentum Adaptativo + RÃ©gimen Gate.
  443: 
  444:     NO ML model. Signal = multi-horizon momentum (ret_10d/20d/50d).
  445:     Architecture:
  446:       Layer 1: Regime gate (Hurst) â†’ decides WHETHER to trade
  447:       Layer 2: Momentum signal â†’ decides WHICH direction
  448:       Layer 3: Execution (Half-Kelly, ATR TP, Effective HS, CB, DL)
  449:     """
  450:     test_start = pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-01-01")
  451:     test_end = pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-12-31")
  452:     test_data = df[(df["date"] >= test_start) & (df["date"] <= test_end)].copy()
  453:     test_data["dow"] = test_data["date"].dt.dayofweek
  454:     mondays = sorted(test_data[test_data["dow"] == 0]["date"].unique())
  455: 
  456:     if not len(mondays):
  457:         return {"trades": [], "equity": 10000.0, "metrics": {},
  458:                 "skipped_weeks": [], "week_data": []}
  459: 
  460:     equity = 10000.0
  461:     peak_equity = 10000.0
  462:     trades = []
  463:     week_data = []
  464:     skipped_weeks = []
  465:     trade_id = 0
  466: 
  467:     # Circuit breaker (v3.0: tighter â€” 4 losses, 10% DD)
  468:     consecutive_losses = 0
  469:     cb_active = False
  470:     cb_activated_at = None
  471:     cb_max_losses = cfg.get("cb_max_consecutive", 4)
  472:     cb_max_dd = cfg.get("cb_max_dd_pct", 10.0)
  473: 
  474:     # Dynamic leverage + regime gate
  475:     recent_pnls = []
  476:     dl_config = cfg["dynamic_leverage"]
  477:     rg_config = cfg["regime_gate"]
  478:     hs_portfolio_cap = cfg.get("effective_hs_portfolio_cap", 0.025)
  479: 
  480:     for monday in mondays:
  481:         monday_ts = pd.Timestamp(monday)
  482:         friday_ts = monday_ts + pd.offsets.BDay(4)
  483: 
  484:         # --- Circuit breaker ---
  485:         # BUG A-2 (auditoria ML 2026-07-21): sin cooldown, el CB era una trampa sin
  486:         # salida â€” los contadores solo sanaban con trades que ya no ocurrian, y el
  487:         # "DEACTIVATED" era codigo muerto. Semantica declarada = pause_and_alert:
  488:         # pausa de 4 semanas, luego re-ancla (peak=equity, losses=0) y reintenta.
  489:         current_dd = (1 - equity / peak_equity) * 100 if peak_equity > 0 else 0
  490:         if cb_active and (monday_ts - cb_activated_at).days >= 28:
  491:             cb_active = False
  492:             consecutive_losses = 0
  493:             peak_equity = equity
  494:             print(f"    [CB] cooldown cumplido at {monday_ts.date()}: re-anclado y reactivado")
  495:         if not cb_active and (consecutive_losses >= cb_max_losses or current_dd >= cb_max_dd):
  496:             cb_active = True
  497:             cb_activated_at = monday_ts
  498:             print(f"    [CB] ACTIVATED at {monday_ts.date()}: "
  499:                   f"consec_losses={consecutive_losses}, DD={current_dd:.1f}%")
  500:         if cb_active:
  501:             skipped_weeks.append({
  502:                 "monday": monday_ts.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
  503:                 "reason": f"circuit_breaker (losses={consecutive_losses}, DD={current_dd:.1f}%)",
  504:             })
  505:             continue
  506: 
  507:         # --- LAYER 1: Regime gate (Hurst) ---
  508:         prior_data = df[df["date"] < monday_ts]
  509:         daily_rets = prior_data["return_1d"].dropna().values
  510:         regime = classify_regime(daily_rets.tolist(), rg_config)
  511: 
  512:         if regime.sizing_factor <= 0:
  513:             skipped_weeks.append({
  514:                 "monday": monday_ts.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
  515:                 "reason": f"regime_gate ({regime.state.value}, H={regime.hurst:.3f})",
  516:             })
  517:             continue
  518: 
  519:         # --- LAYER 2: Momentum signal ---
  520:         close_prices = prior_data["close"].dropna().values
  521:         if len(close_prices) < 52:
  522:             continue
  523: 
  524:         signal = compute_momentum_signal(close_prices, regime.state.value)
  525: 
  526:         if signal.direction is None or signal.confidence == SignalConfidence.SKIP:
  527:             skipped_weeks.append({
  528:                 "monday": monday_ts.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
  529:                 "reason": f"signal_skip ({signal.confidence.value}, "
  530:                           f"ret10d={signal.ret_10d*100:.2f}%, ret20d={signal.ret_20d*100:.2f}%)",
  531:             })
  532:             continue
  533: 
  534:         direction = signal.direction
  535: 
  536:         # --- LAYER 3: Execution ---
  537:         # Vol-targeting
  538:         rets = daily_rets[-21:] if len(daily_rets) >= 21 else daily_rets
  539:         rv_daily = float(np.std(rets)) if len(rets) > 1 else 0.0
  540:         rv_ann = rv_daily * np.sqrt(252) if rv_daily > 0 else cfg["vt_tv"]
  541:         safe_vol = max(rv_ann, cfg["vt_floor"])
  542:         base_lev = np.clip(cfg["vt_tv"] / safe_vol, cfg["vt_min"], cfg["vt_max"])
  543: 
  544:         # Apply signal sizing + regime sizing
  545:         final_lev = np.clip(
  546:             base_lev * signal.sizing_multiplier * regime.sizing_factor,
  547:             cfg["vt_min"], cfg["vt_max"],
  548:         )
  549: 
  550:         # Dynamic leverage
  551:         lookback = dl_config.lookback_weeks
  552:         lev_adj = compute_leverage_adjustment(
  553:             recent_pnls[-lookback:] if len(recent_pnls) >= 3 else [],
  554:             current_dd, dl_config,
  555:         )
  556:         final_lev = np.clip(final_lev * lev_adj, cfg["vt_min"], cfg["vt_max"])
  557: 
  558:         # Adaptive stops
  559:         stops = compute_adaptive_stops(rv_ann, cfg["stops"])
  560: 
  561:         # Effective hard stop (portfolio cap at 2.5%)
  562:         if final_lev > 0:
  563:             effective_hs = min(stops.hard_stop_pct, hs_portfolio_cap / final_lev)
  564:             effective_tp = effective_hs * cfg["stops"].tp_ratio
  565:         else:
  566:             effective_hs = stops.hard_stop_pct
  567:             effective_tp = stops.take_profit_pct
  568: 
  569:         # Entry = Monday close
  570:         monday_row = df[df["date"] == monday_ts]
  571:         if monday_row.empty:
  572:             m2 = df["date"] >= monday_ts
  573:             if m2.any():
  574:                 monday_row = df[m2].iloc[:1]
  575:             else:
  576:                 continue
  577:         entry = float(monday_row["close"].iloc[0])
  578:         entry_date = pd.Timestamp(monday_row["date"].iloc[0])
  579: 
  580:         # Week bars = Tue-Fri
  581:         week_dates = pd.bdate_range(monday_ts + timedelta(days=1), friday_ts)
  582:         bars = []
  583:         last_bar_date = entry_date
  584:         for day in week_dates:
  585:             r = df[df["date"] == day]
  586:             if not r.empty:
  587:                 bars.append({
  588:                     "open": float(r["open"].iloc[0]),
  589:                     "high": float(r["high"].iloc[0]),
  590:                     "low": float(r["low"].iloc[0]),
  591:                     "close": float(r["close"].iloc[0]),
  592:                 })
  593:                 last_bar_date = pd.Timestamp(r["date"].iloc[0])
  594:         if not bars:
  595:             continue
  596: 
  597:         # Simulate
  598:         exit_p, reason, exit_bar_idx = simulate_week(
  599:             direction, entry, bars, effective_hs, effective_tp,
  600:         )
  601: 
  602:         pnl = compute_pnl(direction, entry, exit_p, final_lev, reason,
  603:                            cfg["maker_fee"], cfg["slippage"])
  604: 
  605:         equity_at_entry = equity
  606:         equity *= (1 + pnl)
  607:         peak_equity = max(peak_equity, equity)
  608:         recent_pnls.append(pnl * 100)
  609: 
  610:         # Circuit breaker update
  611:         if pnl < 0:
  612:             consecutive_losses += 1
  613:         else:
  614:             consecutive_losses = 0
  615:             if cb_active:
  616:                 cb_active = False
  617:                 print(f"    [CB] DEACTIVATED at {monday_ts.date()}: positive trade")
  618: 
  619:         trade_id += 1
  620:         exit_dates = [d for d in week_dates if not df[df["date"] == d].empty]
  621:         exit_date = exit_dates[min(exit_bar_idx, len(exit_dates) - 1)] if exit_dates else last_bar_date
  622: 
  623:         trade_dict = {
  624:             "trade_id": trade_id,
  625:             "timestamp": datetime(entry_date.year, entry_date.month, entry_date.day,
  626:                                   9, 0, 0, tzinfo=COT).isoformat(),
  627:             "exit_timestamp": datetime(exit_date.year, exit_date.month, exit_date.day,
  628:                                        12, 50, 0, tzinfo=COT).isoformat(),
  629:             "side": "SHORT" if direction == -1 else "LONG",
  630:             "entry_price": round(entry, 2),
  631:             "exit_price": round(exit_p, 2),
  632:             "pnl_usd": round(equity - equity_at_entry, 2),
  633:             "pnl_pct": round(pnl * 100, 4),
  634:             "exit_reason": reason,
  635:             "equity_at_entry": round(equity_at_entry, 2),
  636:             "equity_at_exit": round(equity, 2),
  637:             "leverage": round(float(final_lev), 3),
  638:             "confidence_tier": signal.confidence.value,
  639:             "hard_stop_pct": round(effective_hs * 100, 2),
  640:             "take_profit_pct": round(effective_tp * 100, 2),
  641:             "regime": regime.state.value,
  642:             "hurst": round(regime.hurst, 3),
  643:         }
  644:         trades.append(trade_dict)
  645: 
  646:         if collect_week_data:
  647:             week_data.append({
  648:                 "signal_date": monday_ts.date() if hasattr(monday_ts, 'date') else monday_ts,
  649:                 "direction": direction,
  650:                 "ret_10d": round(signal.ret_10d * 100, 4),
  651:                 "ret_20d": round(signal.ret_20d * 100, 4),
  652:                 "ret_50d": round(signal.ret_50d * 100, 4),
  653:                 "rv_ann": float(rv_ann),
  654:                 "base_lev": float(base_lev),
  655:                 "final_lev": float(final_lev),
  656:                 "confidence": signal.confidence.value,
  657:                 "sizing_mult": float(signal.sizing_multiplier),
  658:                 "hard_stop_pct": effective_hs,
  659:                 "take_profit_pct": effective_tp,
  660:                 "entry_price": round(entry, 2),
  661:                 "lev_adjustment": round(lev_adj, 3),
  662:                 "regime": regime.state.value,
  663:                 "hurst": round(regime.hurst, 3),
  664:                 "trade": trade_dict,
  665:             })
  666: 
  667:     result = _compute_result_metrics(trades, equity, df, year)
  668:     result["skipped_weeks"] = skipped_weeks
  669:     result["week_data"] = week_data
  670:     return result
  673: def _run_v2_ridge_gate_loop(df, feature_cols, cfg, year, collect_week_data=False):
  674:     """
  675:     Smart Simple v2.0: Ridge+BR+XGBoost + Regime Gate + Effective HS.
  676:     Best balance: +17.59% Sharpe 3.21 in 2025, +0.38% in 2026.
  677:     """
  678:     test_start = pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-01-01")
  679:     test_end = pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-12-31")
  680:     test_data = df[(df["date"] >= test_start) & (df["date"] <= test_end)].copy()
  681:     test_data["dow"] = test_data["date"].dt.dayofweek
  682:     mondays = sorted(test_data[test_data["dow"] == 0]["date"].unique())
  683: 
  684:     if not len(mondays):
  685:         return {"trades": [], "equity": 10000.0, "metrics": {},
  686:                 "skipped_weeks": [], "week_data": []}
  687: 
  688:     # Try XGBoost
  689:     xgb_cls = None
  690:     if cfg.get("use_xgboost", False):  # fail-closed to the live set (see smart_simple_v1.yaml:196-199)
  691:         try:
  692:             from xgboost import XGBRegressor
  693:             xgb_cls = XGBRegressor
  694:         except ImportError:
  695:             pass
  696: 
  697:     equity = 10000.0
  698:     peak_equity = 10000.0
  699:     trades, week_data, skipped_weeks = [], [], []
  700:     trade_id = 0
  701:     consecutive_losses, cb_active = 0, False
  702:     cb_max_losses = cfg.get("cb_max_consecutive", 5)
  703:     cb_max_dd = cfg.get("cb_max_dd_pct", 12.0)
  704:     recent_pnls = []
  705:     dl_config = cfg["dynamic_leverage"]
  706:     rg_config = cfg["regime_gate"]
  707:     hs_cap = cfg.get("effective_hs_portfolio_cap", 0.035)
  708: 
  709:     # H-V13-QRISK-01: probabilistic weekly leverage ceiling. With the flag False (default)
  710:     # week_cap == cfg["vt_max"] every week -> numerics BIT-IDENTICAL to the pre-flag engine.
  711:     v13_enabled = bool(cfg.get("v13_ceiling_enabled", False))
  712:     v13_ceilings = None
  713:     if v13_enabled:
  714:         v13_ceilings = cfg.get("_v13_ceiling_cache")
  715:         if v13_ceilings is None:
  716:             v13_ceilings, v13_meta = compute_v13_leverage_ceilings(df)
  717:             cfg["_v13_ceiling_cache"] = v13_ceilings   # same df across calls in one process
  718:             print(f"    [v13] ceilings: {v13_meta['n_weeks']} weeks, mean={v13_meta['ceiling_mean']:.3f}, "
  719:                   f"min={v13_meta['ceiling_min']:.3f}, qr_fallback={v13_meta['n_qr_fallback']}, "
  720:                   f"inactive={v13_meta['n_ceiling_inactive']}, med_design={v13_meta['median_design_final']:.3f}")
  721: 
  722:     for monday in mondays:
  723:         monday_ts = pd.Timestamp(monday)
  724:         friday_ts = monday_ts + pd.offsets.BDay(4)
  725:         current_dd = (1 - equity / peak_equity) * 100 if peak_equity > 0 else 0
  726: 
  727:         # Circuit breaker
  728:         if consecutive_losses >= cb_max_losses or current_dd >= cb_max_dd:
  729:             if not cb_active:
  730:                 cb_active = True
  731:                 print(f"    [CB] ACTIVATED at {monday_ts.date()}: losses={consecutive_losses}, DD={current_dd:.1f}%")
  732:             skipped_weeks.append({"monday": monday_ts.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
  733:                                   "reason": f"circuit_breaker (losses={consecutive_losses}, DD={current_dd:.1f}%)"})
  734:             continue
  735: 
  736:         # Regime gate
  737:         prior = df[df["date"] < monday_ts]
  738:         daily_rets = prior["return_1d"].dropna().values
  739:         regime = classify_regime(daily_rets.tolist(), rg_config)
  740:         if regime.sizing_factor <= 0:
  741:             skipped_weeks.append({"monday": monday_ts.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
  742:                                   "reason": f"regime_gate ({regime.state.value}, H={regime.hurst:.3f})"})
  743:             continue
  744: 
  745:         # Train Ridge+BR (+XGBoost) on expanding window
  746:         train_end = monday_ts - timedelta(days=1)
  747:         # PURGE (auditoria ML 2026-07-21, BUG A-1): el label de la fila en fecha d usa
  748:         # cierres hasta d+5bd. Sin purga, las ~5 ultimas filas llevan el DESENLACE de la
  749:         # semana que se va a operar dentro del train (y la fila de prediccion se ajustaba
  750:         # con su propio label). Produccion nunca ve esos labels (NaN el domingo) -> el
  751:         # backtest era mas optimista que el modelo servido. Se purga d > train_end - 7d
  752:         # calendario (~5 habiles) del FIT; las features de prediccion se toman aparte.
  753:         purge_end = train_end - timedelta(days=7)
  754:         df_train = df[(df["date"] <= purge_end) & df["target_return_5d"].notna()].copy()
  755:         # Build the feature matrix as a numpy float64 array up front. Coercing via
  756:         # pd.to_numeric first neutralises object/nullable-extension dtypes that can
  757:         # slip in on the extended (2026) slice â€” np.isfinite raises TypeError on a
  758:         # DataFrame with such dtypes, whereas it is well-defined on a float ndarray.
  759:         # np.isfinite is False for NaN, so this single mask subsumes the old notna check.
  760:         feats = df_train[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
  761:         mask = np.isfinite(feats).all(axis=1)
  762:         df_train = df_train[mask]
  763:         feats = feats[mask]
  764:         if len(df_train) < 100:
  765:             continue
  766: 
  767:         X = feats
  768:         y = df_train["target_return_5d"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
  769:         scaler = StandardScaler()
  770:         Xs = scaler.fit_transform(X)
  771:         ridge = Ridge(alpha=1.0).fit(Xs, y)
  772:         br = BayesianRidge(max_iter=300).fit(Xs, y)
  773: 
  774:         # Fila de prediccion: la ULTIMA fila con features finitas hasta train_end (el
  775:         # viernes previo), tomada del frame SIN purga â€” la purga saca esa fila del fit
  776:         # (su label solapa la semana operada) pero sus features siguen siendo las
  777:         # correctas para predecir. Exactamente lo que ve el DAG del domingo.
  778:         df_pred = df[df["date"] <= train_end]
  779:         pred_feats = df_pred[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
  780:         pred_mask = np.isfinite(pred_feats).all(axis=1)
  781:         if not pred_mask.any():
  782:             continue
  783:         latest = scaler.transform(pred_feats[pred_mask][-1:])
  784:         pr = float(ridge.predict(latest)[0])
  785:         pbr = float(br.predict(latest)[0])
  786: 
  787:         pxgb = None
  788:         if xgb_cls:
  789:             try:
  790:                 xgb = xgb_cls(max_depth=3, n_estimators=100, learning_rate=0.1,
  791:                               min_child_weight=10, subsample=0.8, colsample_bytree=0.8,
  792:                               random_state=42, verbosity=0)
  793:                 xgb.fit(Xs, y)
  794:                 pxgb = float(xgb.predict(latest)[0])
  795:             except Exception:
  796:                 pxgb = None
  797: 
  798:         ensemble = (pr + pbr + pxgb) / 3.0 if pxgb is not None else (pr + pbr) / 2.0
  799:         direction = 1 if ensemble > 0 else -1
  800: 
  801:         # Confidence scoring
  802:         conf = score_confidence(pr, pbr, direction, cfg["conf"])
  803:         if conf.skip_trade:
  804:             skipped_weeks.append({"monday": monday_ts.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
  805:                                   "reason": f"low_confidence_{('LONG' if direction==1 else 'SHORT')}"})
  806:             continue
  807: 
  808:         # Vol-targeting + sizing
  809:         # week_cap: static vt_max, or (v13) the sealed probabilistic ceiling for this Monday.
  810:         # Missing key -> fail-safe to vt_max (a v13 config runs with vt_max=1.5, and the
  811:         # ceiling never exceeds 1.5, so the fail-safe can only be MORE conservative than v11).
  812:         week_cap = cfg["vt_max"]
  813:         if v13_enabled:
  814:             week_cap = float(v13_ceilings.get(monday_ts.normalize(), cfg["vt_max"]))
  815:         rets = df_train["return_1d"].dropna().values
  816:         rv_daily = np.std(rets[-21:]) if len(rets) >= 21 else 0.0
  817:         rv_ann = rv_daily * np.sqrt(252) if rv_daily > 0 else cfg["vt_tv"]
  818:         safe_vol = max(rv_ann, cfg["vt_floor"])
  819:         base_lev = np.clip(cfg["vt_tv"] / safe_vol, cfg["vt_min"], week_cap)
  820:         final_lev = np.clip(base_lev * conf.sizing_multiplier, cfg["vt_min"], week_cap)
  821: 
  822:         # Dynamic leverage
  823:         lev_adj = compute_leverage_adjustment(
  824:             recent_pnls[-dl_config.lookback_weeks:] if len(recent_pnls) >= 3 else [],
  825:             current_dd, dl_config)
  826:         final_lev = np.clip(final_lev * lev_adj * regime.sizing_factor, cfg["vt_min"], week_cap)
  827: 
  828:         # Stops
  829:         stops = compute_adaptive_stops(rv_ann, cfg["stops"])
  830:         if final_lev > 0:
  831:             effective_hs = min(stops.hard_stop_pct, hs_cap / final_lev)
  832:             effective_tp = effective_hs * cfg["stops"].tp_ratio
  833:         else:
  834:             effective_hs, effective_tp = stops.hard_stop_pct, stops.take_profit_pct
  835: 
  836:         # Entry
  837:         monday_row = df[df["date"] == monday_ts]
  838:         if monday_row.empty:
  839:             m2 = df["date"] >= monday_ts
  840:             if m2.any():
  841:                 monday_row = df[m2].iloc[:1]
  842:             else:
  843:                 continue
  844:         entry = float(monday_row["close"].iloc[0])
  845:         entry_date = pd.Timestamp(monday_row["date"].iloc[0])
  846: 
  847:         # Week bars
  848:         week_dates = pd.bdate_range(monday_ts + timedelta(days=1), friday_ts)
  849:         bars, last_bar_date = [], entry_date
  850:         for day in week_dates:
  851:             r = df[df["date"] == day]
  852:             if not r.empty:
  853:                 bars.append({"open": float(r["open"].iloc[0]),
  854:                              "high": float(r["high"].iloc[0]), "low": float(r["low"].iloc[0]),
  855:                              "close": float(r["close"].iloc[0])})
  856:                 last_bar_date = pd.Timestamp(r["date"].iloc[0])
  857:         if not bars:
  858:             continue
  859: 
  860:         if cfg.get("ladder_enabled"):
  861:             exit_p, reason, exit_bar_idx = simulate_week_ladder(
  862:                 direction, entry, bars, effective_hs, effective_tp, cfg.get("ladder_k2", 2.0))
  863:         else:
  864:             exit_p, reason, exit_bar_idx = simulate_week(direction, entry, bars, effective_hs, effective_tp)
  865:         pnl = compute_pnl(direction, entry, exit_p, final_lev, reason, cfg["maker_fee"], cfg["slippage"])
  866: 
  867:         equity_at_entry = equity
  868:         equity *= (1 + pnl)
  869:         peak_equity = max(peak_equity, equity)
  870:         recent_pnls.append(pnl * 100)
  871: 
  872:         if pnl < 0:
  873:             consecutive_losses += 1
  874:         else:
  875:             consecutive_losses = 0
  876:             if cb_active:
  877:                 cb_active = False
  878: 
  879:         trade_id += 1
  880:         exit_dates = [d for d in week_dates if not df[df["date"] == d].empty]
  881:         exit_date = exit_dates[min(exit_bar_idx, len(exit_dates) - 1)] if exit_dates else last_bar_date
  882: 
  883:         tier_val = conf.tier.value if hasattr(conf.tier, 'value') else str(conf.tier)
  884:         trade_dict = {
  885:             "trade_id": trade_id,
  886:             "timestamp": datetime(entry_date.year, entry_date.month, entry_date.day, 9, 0, 0, tzinfo=COT).isoformat(),
  887:             "exit_timestamp": datetime(exit_date.year, exit_date.month, exit_date.day, 12, 50, 0, tzinfo=COT).isoformat(),
  888:             "side": "SHORT" if direction == -1 else "LONG",
  889:             "entry_price": round(entry, 2), "exit_price": round(exit_p, 2),
  890:             "pnl_usd": round(equity - equity_at_entry, 2), "pnl_pct": round(pnl * 100, 4),
  891:             "exit_reason": reason,
  892:             "equity_at_entry": round(equity_at_entry, 2), "equity_at_exit": round(equity, 2),
  893:             "leverage": round(float(final_lev), 3), "confidence_tier": tier_val,
  894:             "hard_stop_pct": round(effective_hs * 100, 2), "take_profit_pct": round(effective_tp * 100, 2),
  895:             "regime": regime.state.value, "hurst": round(regime.hurst, 3),
  896:         }
  897:         trades.append(trade_dict)
  898:         if collect_week_data:
  899:             week_data.append({
  900:                 "signal_date": monday_ts.date(), "direction": direction,
  901:                 "pred_ridge": pr, "pred_br": pbr, "pred_xgb": pxgb, "ensemble": ensemble,
  902:                 "rv_ann": float(rv_ann), "base_lev": float(base_lev), "final_lev": float(final_lev),
  903:                 "confidence_tier": tier_val, "sizing_mult": float(conf.sizing_multiplier),
  904:                 "hard_stop_pct": effective_hs, "take_profit_pct": effective_tp,
  905:                 "entry_price": round(entry, 2), "lev_adjustment": round(lev_adj, 3),
  906:                 "regime": regime.state.value, "hurst": round(regime.hurst, 3), "trade": trade_dict,
  907:             })
  908: 
  909:     result = _compute_result_metrics(trades, equity, df, year)
  910:     result["skipped_weeks"] = skipped_weeks
  911:     result["week_data"] = week_data
  912:     return result
  913: 
  914: 
  915: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  916: # Version selector
  917: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  918: 
  919: STRATEGY_VERSIONS = {
  920:     "v2": _run_v2_ridge_gate_loop,    # Ridge+BR+XGB + Gate + HS (production)
  921:     "v3": _run_weekly_loop,            # Momentum + Gate (paper trading)
  922: }
  923: 
  924: 
  925: def run_walkforward_backtest(df, feature_cols, cfg, year, version="v2"):
  926:     """Walk-forward backtest with version selector."""
  927:     fn = STRATEGY_VERSIONS.get(version, _run_v2_ridge_gate_loop)
  928:     return fn(df, feature_cols, cfg, year, collect_week_data=False)
  929: 
  930: 
  931: def run_production_backtest(df, feature_cols, cfg, year, version="v2"):
  932:     """Production backtest with version selector."""
  933:     fn = STRATEGY_VERSIONS.get(version, _run_v2_ridge_gate_loop)
  934:     return fn(df, feature_cols, cfg, year, collect_week_data=True)
  935: 
  936: 
  937: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  938: # Shared metrics
  939: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
